The COMP-012 probe at\n``tests/cli/eval/test_isolation_layers_probe.py`` (Task T02.05) must keep\npassing after this extension, which it does because nothing in\n``cli/sprint/executor.py`` is touched.\n\nDM-006 record (T02.04) — the four immutable fields\n====================================================\n\n``HomeIsolation`` is a ``@dataclass(frozen=True)`` so once an instance is\nbuilt it cannot be mutated by downstream consumers. This makes it safe to\nshare across threads inside the parallel orchestrator (R-058 / T03.16)\nwithout locking. Equality is structural across all four fields via the\ndataclass-generated ``__eq__``.\n\n1. ``eval_id``         — the FR-SCH2-validated eval identifier. The id\n   flows directly into the per-eval HOME by :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup`\n   so it MUST satisfy the FR-SCH2 regex before any filesystem write. The\n   ``__post_init__`` hook re-applies :func:`validate_eval_id` (T01.05) so\n   a ``HomeIsolation`` can never be constructed with an unsafe id even\n   when a caller bypasses the loader.\n2. ``home_root``       — the scratch root directory under which the\n   per-eval HOME is ``mkdtemp``-ed by :meth:`setup`. Full FR-ISO2 path\n   containment lands in T02.08; this module performs only the minimal\n   ``parents=True, exist_ok=True`` materialization needed to create\n   sibling per-eval HOMEs concurrently.\n3. ``session_id``      — the value :meth:`env` stamps into\n   ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID``. Allocation lives in the orchestrator (FR-G2 /\n   T03.16); this record only holds the assigned value.\n4. ``time_offset_sec`` — optional offset in whole seconds for the eval's\n   simulated wall clock. Default ``0`` matches DM-006 verbatim; when the\n   value is non-zero :meth:`env` adds ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET``. Whether\n   the subprocess actually honors the variable is gated on OQ-8 (DOC-OQ8\n   / T06.03); keeping the offset opt-in via non-zero values means the\n   env dict stays minimal until OQ-8 resolves.\n\nCOMP-006 method surface (T02.07) — FR-ISO1\n==========================================\n\nThe four methods below are the externally observable surface of\nCOMP-006. They mutate process-local filesystem state (and an internal\nprivate slot tracking the created HOME path) without ever mutating the\nfour DM-006 fields:\n\n* :meth:`setup`                 — ``mkdtemp`` under ``home_root`` and stash\n  the created path on a private ``_home_path`` slot via\n  ``object.__setattr__``. Returns the created path. Idempotency is one-\n  shot per instance: calling :meth:`setup` twice raises ``RuntimeError``\n  rather than silently leaking a directory.\n* :meth:`env`                   — returns the env-var dict the subprocess\n  needs: ``HOME`` always, ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` always, and\n  ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` only when ``time_offset_sec != 0``. The\n  return type pins ``dict[str, str]`` so callers can ``.update()`` it\n  alongside ``IsolationLayers.env_vars`` without coercion.\n* :meth:`teardown`              — removes the per-eval HOME when ``keep``\n  is false, leaves it on disk when ``keep`` is true (so post-mortem\n  inspection or NFR-ISO2 ``setup_failed`` tagging in T02.13 can still\n  pick the directory up). Always clears the private path slot so the\n  instance is safe to garbage-collect.\n* :meth:`state_path`            — joins ``suffix`` under the active\n  per-eval HOME so consumers (hook adapter T02.14, atomic setup T02.13,\n  artifact writers) never need to import :mod:`os.path` themselves. The\n  full containment check on ``suffix`` lands in T02.08; this method\n  performs the minimum necessary check that the joined path stays under\n  ``home_path``.\n\nCross-links\n===========\n\n* DM-006 record spec (roadmap row 26).\n* COMP-006 method surface (roadmap rows 28 / 32; T02.07 / T02.11).\n* FR-SCH2 :func:`validate_eval_id` (T01.05).\n* FR-ISO2 ``containment_guard`` (T02.08) — strengthens the symlink /\n  allowlist / regex checks layered on top of :meth:`setup` and\n  :meth:`state_path`.\n* DOC-OQ8 (T06.03) — gates ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` semantics.\n* COMP-012 probe (T02.05 — ``test_isolation_layers_probe.py``) — pins the\n  upstream ``IsolationLayers`` shape this module sits beside.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nimport tempfile\nimport traceback\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom .config import EvalConfig, ScratchRootViolation, resolve_scratch_root\nfrom .loader import InvalidEvalId, validate_eval_id\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"SETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH\",\n    \"HomeContainmentViolation\",\n    \"HomeIsolation\",\n    \"containment_guard\",\n]\n\n\n# NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup contract — see ``HomeIsolation.setup`` docstring and\n# D-0033/spec.md. The tag is written under the per-eval HOME so that the\n# directory becomes self-describing for downstream consumers (the orchestrator,\n# the EvalRunner, post-mortem operators): a HOME that carries this relative\n# path is one whose setup raised AFTER ``tempfile.mkdtemp`` materialized the\n# directory and BEFORE ``setup`` returned successfully. The constant is\n# exported so :class:`EvalRunner` (T03.x) and forensics tooling can locate the\n# tag without re-deriving the path.\nSETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH = \".eval-meta/setup_failed\"\n\n\ndef _write_setup_failed_tag(home_path: Path, exc: BaseException) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Write the NFR-ISO2 ``setup_failed`` artifact tag under ``home_path``.\n\n    The tag distinguishes \"harness bug or environmental refusal during\n    setup\" (status ERRORED) from \"eval ran and produced a failing\n    assertion\" (status FAIL). The contents are split for grep-ability:\n\n    * Line 1 is the exception class name verbatim\n      (``type(exc).__name__``), so a downstream EvalRunner can bucket\n      the error with a single ``head -n1`` read.\n    * The remainder is the formatted traceback for forensic inspection.\n\n    The helper is best-effort: ``setup`` catches and discards any\n    exception this function raises so a write failure (e.g. the\n    ``.eval-meta`` directory cannot be created because the per-eval HOME\n    was made read-only by a future hardening step) never masks the\n    original setup exception.\n\n    Args:\n        home_path: The per-eval HOME ``mkdtemp``-ed by\n            :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup`. The tag is materialized at\n            ``home_path / SETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH``.\n        exc: The exception that interrupted ``setup``. Used only to\n            populate the tag contents; this helper never re-raises it.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    tag_path = home_path / SETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH\n    tag_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    # ``traceback.format_exception`` reads the active exception state when\n    # the helper is invoked from inside an ``except`` clause. Pin the\n    # arguments explicitly so the helper also works when called outside\n    # one (defensive — the only call site is currently inside ``except``).\n    tb_lines = traceback.format_exception(type(exc), exc, exc.__traceback__)\n    payload = f\"{type(exc).__name__}\\n\" + \"\".join(tb_lines)\n    tag_path.write_text(payload, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\nclass HomeContainmentViolation(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised by :func:`containment_guard` when any FR-ISO2 check fails.\n\n    The exception is the single hard-failure surface of FR-ISO2 — it is\n    the only thing :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` may raise once\n    :func:`tempfile.mkdtemp` has succeeded and BEFORE the hook adapter\n    (T02.14) writes a single byte under the per-eval HOME. Catching this\n    type at the orchestrator level is sufficient to cover all three\n    containment checks (eval_id regex, scratch-root allowlist, post-\n    mkdtemp symlink resolution); callers MUST NOT branch on the\n    underlying :class:`InvalidEvalId` / :class:`ScratchRootViolation`\n    causes — they are exposed via ``__cause__`` for forensics only.\n\n    The exception MUST raise before any hook deploy or eval-state\n    write so NFR-SEC2 (\"hard refusal before side effects\") is upheld;\n    the NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) is responsible for\n    preserving the partial per-eval HOME with a ``setup_failed`` tag\n    once it lands.\n\n    Attributes:\n        check: Short identifier for the failed check (``\"eval_id\"``,\n            ``\"scratch_root_allowlist\"``, ``\"home_path_resolution\"`` or\n            ``\"home_path_escape\"``). Lets reporters bucket failures\n            without parsing the human-readable message.\n        home_path: The per-eval HOME path the guard was asked to\n            validate. Stored verbatim as supplied by the caller (no\n            resolution) so forensics keep the original input.\n        scratch_root: The declared scratch root the guard checked\n            ``home_path`` against. Stored verbatim.\n        eval_id: The eval id under inspection. Stored verbatim\n            (``repr``-rendered) so non-string smuggled scalars are\n            visible in reporter output.\n        detail: Free-form one-line message describing the specific\n            failure. Inherits from the underlying cause when one\n            exists (e.g. the ``ScratchRootViolation`` rendering).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        check: str,\n        home_path: Path,\n        scratch_root: Path,\n        eval_id: object,\n        detail: str,\n    ) -> None:\n        self.check = check\n        self.home_path = home_path\n        self.scratch_root = scratch_root\n        self.eval_id = eval_id\n        self.detail = detail\n        super().__init__(\n            f\"FR-ISO2 containment check {check!r} failed for \"\n            f\"eval_id={eval_id!r} home_path={home_path!s} \"\n            f\"scratch_root={scratch_root!s}: {detail}\"\n        )\n\n\ndef containment_guard(\n    home_path: Path,\n    scratch_root: Path,\n    eval_id: str,\n    *,\n    config: EvalConfig,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"FR-ISO2 three-check defense-in-depth guard.\n\n    The guard layers three independent checks on top of the per-eval\n    HOME materialization that :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` just performed.\n    Each check has a distinct attack vector in scope:\n\n    1. **eval_id regex** — re-applies :func:`validate_eval_id` (T01.05).\n       The loader (T01.07) already validates the id pre-expand and\n       post-expand, but a caller bypassing :class:`SuiteLoader` (e.g.,\n       a programmatic test, a future REPL entry point) could construct\n       a :class:`HomeIsolation` directly. The re-check closes that\n       loader-bypass surface called out in NFR-SEC2.\n    2. **Scratch-root allowlist** — re-applies\n       :func:`resolve_scratch_root` (T01.19) against ``scratch_root``.\n       The helper resolves the path with ``Path.resolve(strict=False)``\n       which collapses symlinks; if ``scratch_root`` is a symlink to a\n       non-allowlisted target (e.g., ``/home/user/.claude``) the\n       resolved form will not match any entry in the resolved\n       :attr:`EvalConfig.allowed_scratch_roots`. The allowlist is\n       sourced exclusively from the caller-supplied\n       :class:`EvalConfig`; nothing inside this module synthesizes a\n       fallback that could inject the caller's ``scratch_root``\n       into the allowlist after the fact (NFR-SEC2 loader-bypass\n       defense — see D-0029 spec rationale).\n    3. **Post-mkdtemp path containment** — resolves ``home_path`` with\n       ``Path.resolve(strict=True)`` and asserts the resolved form is\n       equal to OR a sub-path of the resolved ``scratch_root``. The\n       ``strict=True`` mode raises :class:`FileNotFoundError` if the\n       per-eval HOME has not been materialized yet, so this is the\n       check that pins \"guard runs AFTER ``mkdtemp``\". Catches the\n       attack vector where a symlinked component inside ``home_path``\n       points outside ``scratch_root`` even though the textual prefix\n       check would have passed.\n\n    On any failure the function raises :class:`HomeContainmentViolation`\n    with the failing check identifier. Underlying causes\n    (:class:`InvalidEvalId`, :class:`ScratchRootViolation`,\n    :class:`FileNotFoundError`) are chained via ``raise ... from`` so\n    forensic tooling can still walk ``__cause__``.\n\n    Args:\n        home_path: Path to the per-eval HOME directory ``mkdtemp``-ed by\n            :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup`. Must exist on disk; the\n            ``strict=True`` resolve enforces this so a caller cannot\n            invoke the guard \"ahead\" of ``mkdtemp``.\n        scratch_root: Scratch root under which ``home_path`` must\n            reside. Need not exist (``strict=False`` resolution); for\n            test ergonomics callers can pass a path that\n            :class:`HomeIsolation` will create via\n            ``mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)`` shortly after.\n        eval_id: The eval identifier the per-eval HOME corresponds to.\n            Re-validated through :func:`validate_eval_id`.\n        config: Required :class:`EvalConfig`. Its\n            ``allowed_scratch_roots`` is the sole source of truth for\n            the scratch-root allowlist check. The guard refuses to\n            synthesize a fallback config: a missing argument is a\n            programming error, never a \"use the defaults\" cue. This\n            closes the bypass where a caller could otherwise construct\n            ``HomeIsolation(home_root=<attacker-controlled>)`` and have\n            the guard quietly add that root to the allowlist.\n\n    Raises:\n        HomeContainmentViolation: Any of the three checks failed. The\n            ``check`` attribute identifies which one; ``detail`` carries\n            the human-readable cause; ``__cause__`` holds the underlying\n            exception when applicable.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Check 1: eval_id regex. Loader-bypass defense.\n    try:\n        validate_eval_id(eval_id)\n    except InvalidEvalId as exc:\n        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n            check=\"eval_id\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n            eval_id=eval_id,\n            detail=str(exc),\n        ) from exc\n\n    # Check 2: scratch-root allowlist (symlink-aware via resolve_scratch_root).\n    # ``config`` is required: see docstring rationale (NFR-SEC2 bypass close).\n    try:\n        resolved_scratch = resolve_scratch_root(scratch_root, config=config)\n    except ScratchRootViolation as exc:\n        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n            check=\"scratch_root_allowlist\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n            eval_id=eval_id,\n            detail=str(exc),\n        ) from exc\n\n    # Check 3: post-mkdtemp symlink resolution + containment under\n    # scratch_root. strict=True asserts the HOME exists (i.e., mkdtemp\n    # already ran). Any symlink inside home_path is collapsed; the\n    # resolved form must equal or live beneath the resolved scratch root.\n    try:\n        resolved_home = home_path.resolve(strict=True)\n    except FileNotFoundError as exc:\n        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n            check=\"home_path_resolution\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n            eval_id=eval_id,\n            detail=(\n                \"home_path does not exist on disk; FR-ISO2 requires the \"\n                \"guard to run AFTER mkdtemp materialized the per-eval \"\n                \"HOME\"\n            ),\n        ) from exc\n\n    if not (\n        resolved_home == resolved_scratch\n        or resolved_home.is_relative_to(resolved_scratch)\n    ):\n        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n            check=\"home_path_escape\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n            eval_id=eval_id,\n            detail=(\n                f\"resolved home_path {resolved_home!s} escapes resolved \"\n                f\"scratch_root {resolved_scratch!s}\"\n            ),\n        )\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass HomeIsolation:\n    \"\"\"Per-eval HOME isolation record + COMP-006 method surface.\n\n    DM-006 fixes the immutable four-field record contract:\n\n    * ``eval_id``        — ``str``, required. Re-validated in\n      ``__post_init__`` via :func:`validate_eval_id` so any unsafe id\n      raises :class:`superclaude.cli.eval.InvalidEvalId` BEFORE any\n      consumer can derive a filesystem path from it.\n    * ``home_root``      — ``pathlib.Path``, required. Scratch root under\n      which the per-eval HOME is created by :meth:`setup`. The dataclass\n      stores it as-is; full containment is FR-ISO2's responsibility\n      (T02.08).\n    * ``session_id``     — ``str``, required. Value :meth:`env` stamps\n      into ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` for the subprocess. Allocation lives in\n      the orchestrator (FR-G2 / T03.16); this record only holds the\n      assigned value.\n    * ``time_offset_sec`` — ``int``, defaults to ``0``. Optional simulated\n      wall-clock offset in whole seconds; :meth:`env` emits\n      ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` only when non-zero. Full activation is\n      gated on OQ-8 (DOC-OQ8 / T06.03).\n\n    The four declared fields are frozen — mutation raises\n    ``dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError``. A single private slot,\n    ``_home_path``, tracks the post-:meth:`setup` HOME directory; it is\n    written via ``object.__setattr__`` (the documented escape hatch for\n    frozen dataclasses) and never participates in equality or hashing.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    eval_id: str\n    home_root: Path\n    session_id: str\n    time_offset_sec: int = 0\n\n    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Re-apply the FR-SCH2 guard and initialize the private path slot.\n\n        The FR-SCH2 re-validation makes loader-bypass impossible:\n        constructing a ``HomeIsolation`` with an unsafe ``eval_id``\n        raises :class:`InvalidEvalId` before any filesystem operation\n        becomes reachable. The private ``_home_path`` slot is set to\n        ``None`` so :meth:`env` / :meth:`state_path` can raise a clear\n        error when called before :meth:`setup`.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        validate_eval_id(self.eval_id)\n        object.__setattr__(self, \"_home_path\", None)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # COMP-006 method surface (FR-ISO1)\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    @property\n    def home_path(self) -> Path:\n        \"\"\"Return the per-eval HOME directory created by :meth:`setup`.\n\n        Raises :class:`RuntimeError` when :meth:`setup` has not yet been\n        called or when :meth:`teardown` has already cleared the slot,\n        so the failure mode is \"obvious AttributeError\" instead of a\n        silent ``None`` flowing into an env dict.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        home = self._home_path  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n        if home is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(\n                \"HomeIsolation.setup() must be called before accessing home_path\"\n            )\n        return home\n\n    @property\n    def is_set_up(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Return ``True`` when :meth:`setup` has created a per-eval HOME\n        and :meth:`teardown` has not yet been called.\n\n        Lets callers (atomic setup wrapper T02.13, the orchestrator)\n        ask whether teardown is needed without catching exceptions.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        return self._home_path is not None  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n\n    def setup(self, *, config: EvalConfig) -> Path:\n        \"\"\"Create the per-eval HOME directory under ``home_root``.\n\n        Uses :func:`tempfile.mkdtemp` with the eval_id as prefix so\n        sibling per-eval HOMEs under the same ``home_root`` are\n        guaranteed to be unique by the OS (atomic on POSIX, see\n        ``mkdtemp`` docs). Parallel evals never collide on directory\n        names; mutating one HOME cannot affect siblings because each\n        HOME is its own ``mkdtemp`` root.\n\n        After ``mkdtemp`` succeeds, :func:`containment_guard` (FR-ISO2,\n        T02.08) is applied AFTER the per-eval HOME has been\n        materialized and BEFORE any hook deploy or eval-state write.\n        The guard re-validates ``eval_id`` against the FR-SCH2 regex,\n        re-applies the AC12 scratch-root allowlist, and\n        ``Path.resolve(strict=True)``-checks the freshly created HOME\n        against the scratch root. Any failure surfaces as\n        :class:`HomeContainmentViolation`; the partial per-eval HOME is\n        left on disk so the NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) can\n        tag it as ``setup_failed`` for forensic inspection.\n\n        Idempotency rule: calling :meth:`setup` twice on the same\n        instance raises :class:`RuntimeError`. This prevents the silent\n        directory leak that would otherwise occur if a caller looped\n        over an instance, and it makes the atomic-setup wrapper\n        (T02.13) easier to reason about.\n\n        Args:\n            config: Required :class:`EvalConfig`. Its\n                ``allowed_scratch_roots`` provides the scratch-root\n                allowlist :func:`containment_guard` enforces.\n                ``setup`` deliberately refuses to synthesize a\n                fallback that injects ``self.home_root`` into the\n                allowlist — that would defeat the AC12 check, because a\n                caller can construct ``HomeIsolation`` with any\n                ``home_root`` (per-instance, the dataclass cannot\n                meaningfully validate it). Production callers\n                (orchestrator T03.16) pass the run's resolved config;\n                tests build an :class:`EvalConfig` whose allowlist\n                includes the test scratch root.\n\n        Returns:\n            The freshly created per-eval HOME path (also recorded on\n            :attr:`home_path`).\n\n        Raises:\n            RuntimeError: If :meth:`setup` was already called on this\n                instance and :meth:`teardown` has not run since.\n            HomeContainmentViolation: Any of the three FR-ISO2 checks\n                failed; the per-eval HOME directory was created and is\n                preserved on disk for inspection.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        if self._home_path is not None:  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n            raise RuntimeError(\n                f\"HomeIsolation.setup() already called for eval_id={self.eval_id!r}\"\n            )\n\n        # Ensure the scratch root exists. ``parents=True, exist_ok=True``\n        # is safe because the FR-ISO2 guard below catches any path that\n        # resolves outside the policy allowlist.\n        self.home_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n        home = Path(\n            tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f\"{self.eval_id}-\", dir=str(self.home_root))\n        )\n        object.__setattr__(self, \"_home_path\", home)\n\n        # FR-ISO2 defense-in-depth: re-check eval_id, scratch-root\n        # allowlist, and symlink-resolved containment AFTER mkdtemp and\n        # BEFORE the hook adapter (T02.14) writes anything under the\n        # per-eval HOME. A failure here leaves the directory on disk so\n        # the NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) can tag it.\n        containment_guard(\n            home_path=home,\n            scratch_root=self.home_root,\n            eval_id=self.eval_id,\n            config=config,\n        )\n        return home\n\n    def env(self) -> dict[str, str]:\n        \"\"\"Return the env-var dict the subprocess needs for HOME isolation.\n\n        * ``HOME`` is always present.\n        * ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` is always present (FR-ISO1 mandate).\n        * ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` is present only when\n          ``time_offset_sec`` differs from the default ``0``. Keeping the\n          variable opt-in means OQ-8 (DOC-OQ8 / T06.03) does not have to\n          be resolved before this method is safe to call: a caller that\n          does not request a time offset gets the historic two-key dict.\n\n        Callers ``.update()`` this dict alongside\n        ``IsolationLayers.env_vars`` to assemble the full subprocess\n        env; the return type is pinned to ``dict[str, str]`` so the\n        merge is type-clean.\n\n        Raises :class:`RuntimeError` if invoked before :meth:`setup`,\n        because the ``HOME`` value would otherwise have to be a\n        fabricated placeholder.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        env: dict[str, str] = {\n            \"HOME\": str(self.home_path),\n            \"CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\": self.session_id,\n        }\n        if self.time_offset_sec != 0:\n            env[\"CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET\"] = str(self.time_offset_sec)\n        return env\n\n    def teardown(self, keep: bool) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Tear down the per-eval HOME directory.\n\n        * ``keep=False`` removes the directory tree (``shutil.rmtree``\n          with ``ignore_errors=False``; any failure surfaces to the\n          caller so the orchestrator can decide whether to flag the\n          eval as cleanup-failed).\n        * ``keep=True`` leaves the directory on disk for post-mortem\n          inspection — this is the branch the NFR-ISO2 atomic setup\n          wrapper (T02.13) takes when ``setup`` partially fails, and the\n          branch :meth:`teardown` is called on under ``--keep-home``\n          (T03.18).\n\n        Either way, the private ``_home_path`` slot is cleared after\n        teardown, so subsequent :meth:`env` / :meth:`state_path` calls\n        fail loudly instead of pointing at a stale (possibly deleted)\n        path. Calling :meth:`teardown` when no HOME has been set up is\n        a no-op — callers are not required to track setup state\n        themselves.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        home = self._home_path  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n        if home is None:\n            return  # No-op: setup never ran, or teardown already ran.\n\n        try:\n            if not keep:\n                shutil.rmtree(home)\n        finally:\n            # Always clear the slot — even if rmtree raised, the path\n            # is no longer trustworthy for future env() / state_path()\n            # consumers and callers will re-run setup() if they want a\n            # fresh HOME.\n            object.__setattr__(self, \"_home_path\", None)\n\n    def state_path(self, suffix: str) -> Path:\n        \"\"\"Return ``home_path / suffix`` after a light containment check.\n\n        ``suffix`` must be a relative path (no leading separator) and\n        must not contain ``..`` components — otherwise the returned\n        path could escape ``home_path`` and undermine the\n        per-eval HOME isolation. Full FR-ISO2 containment\n        (symlink resolution + allowlist) lands in T02.08; this method\n        performs only the minimum check necessary to keep the\n        primitive safe in isolation.\n\n        Raises :class:`ValueError` for any suffix that escapes\n        ``home_path``. Raises :class:`RuntimeError` if invoked before\n        :meth:`setup` (via :attr:`home_path`).\n        \"\"\"\n\n        candidate = Path(suffix)\n        if candidate.is_absolute():\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"state_path suffix must be relative, got absolute path: {suffix!r}\"\n            )\n        if \"..\" in candidate.parts:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"state_path suffix must not contain '..' components: {suffix!r}\"\n            )\n\n        joined = self.home_path / candidate\n        # Defense-in-depth: even with the part check above, refuse any\n        # joined path that does not resolve under home_path. This\n        # complements the FR-ISO2 guard in T02.08 (which will resolve\n        # symlinks); here we work on the lexical path because\n        # state_path may legitimately be called before the suffix\n        # exists on disk.\n        try:\n            joined.relative_to(self.home_path)\n        except ValueError as exc:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"state_path suffix escapes home_path: {suffix!r}\"\n            ) from exc\n        return joined\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":483,"oldLines":7,"newStart":483,"newLines":31,"lines":["                 instance and :meth:`teardown` has not run since.","             HomeContainmentViolation: Any of the three FR-ISO2 checks","                 failed; the per-eval HOME directory was created and is","-                preserved on disk for inspection.","+                preserved on disk for inspection with a","+                ``setup_failed`` tag (NFR-ISO2 — see below).","+","+        NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup contract (T02.13 / D-0033)","+        ------------------------------------------------","+","+        Any exception raised AFTER :func:`tempfile.mkdtemp` and BEFORE","+        ``setup`` returns successfully MUST leave the per-eval HOME on","+        disk and MUST drop a ``setup_failed`` artifact tag at","+        ``<home>/.eval-meta/setup_failed`` (constant","+        :data:`SETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH`). T02.10 closes the loop by exercising the\n16\t*real* ``~/.claude/`` directory on the host — the catastrophic case\n17\tthe operator would actually hit.\n18\t\n19\tHard-guard contract (NFR-SEC3 / T02.10)\n20\t=======================================\n21\t\n22\tThe contract this module pins is:\n23\t\n24\t1. **Refusal surface.** :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` MUST raise\n25\t   :class:`HomeContainmentViolation` whenever the per-eval HOME would\n26\t   resolve to (or inside) the host's real ``~/.claude/`` — either by\n27\t   passing it directly as ``home_root`` or by passing a scratch root\n28\t   that symlink-escapes there.\n29\t2. **Containment bucketing.** The ``check`` identifier on the raised\n30\t   exception MUST be ``\"scratch_root_allowlist\"`` for the \"real\n31\t   ``~/.claude/`` as scratch root\" attack (it fails AC12 before any\n32\t   symlink resolution), and ``\"home_path_escape\"`` when the per-eval\n33\t   HOME materializes as a symlink pointing into ``~/.claude/`` (check 3\n34\t   catches the chain).\n35\t3. **Refusal-before-side-effects on the real HOME.** No file inside the\n36\t   real ``~/.claude/`` is created, modified, or touched as a side\n37\t   effect of the attempted setup. The skip reason names the missing path so a CI\n55\toperator can decide whether to materialize it or accept the skip.\n56\t\n57\tLeak cleanup\n58\t============\n59\t\n60\tThe current :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` design invokes\n61\t``tempfile.mkdtemp`` BEFORE :func:`containment_guard` runs (see D-0029\n62\tspec rationale — the partial HOME is preserved for NFR-ISO2 / T02.13\n63\tto tag as ``setup_failed``). \"\"\"Snapshot ``~/.claude/`` BEFORE the test runs.\n\n    The fixture is consumed by every hard-guard test that needs to\n    prove ``HomeIsolation.setup`` did not write under the real HOME.\n    The cleanup_leaked_eval_homes(real_claude_dir: Path) -> Iterator[None]:\n    \"\"\"Delete any per-eval HOMEs this module leaked into ``~/.claude/``.\n\n    The current ``HomeIsolation.setup`` design invokes\n    ``tempfile.mkdtemp`` BEFORE the FR-ISO2 guard runs (see D-0029\n    spec — partial HOMEs are intentionally preserved on disk so the\n    NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper in T02.13 can tag them as\n    ``setup_failed``). For the \"real ``~/.claude/`` as scratch root\"\n    test that means the harness leaks an *empty* per-eval HOME under\n    the real HOME despite refusing the operation. The fixture removes\n    those directories at teardown so the host's real ``~/.claude/``\n    stays in its pre-test shape.\n\n    Cleanup is strictly scoped to entries whose name starts with the\n    test module's distinctive eval-id prefix (:data:`_EVAL_ID`), so\n    the fixture cannot accidentally delete unrelated content.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    before = {p.name for p in real_claude_dir.iterdir()}\n    yield\n    after = {p.name for p in real_claude_dir.iterdir()}\n    new = after - before\n    leaked_prefix = f\"{_EVAL_ID}-\"\n    for name in new:\n        if not name.startswith(leaked_prefix):\n            continue\n        leaked = real_claude_dir / name\n        if leaked.is_symlink():\n            leaked.unlink()\n        elif leaked.is_dir():\n            shutil.rmtree(leaked)\n        else:\n            leaked.unlink()\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Hard-guard 1 — real ``~/.claude/`` passed directly as ``home_root``\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass TestRealHomeAsScratchRoot:\n    \"\"\"NFR-SEC3 catastrophic case: operator points ``--scratch-root`` at\n    the host's real ``~/.claude/``.\n\n    Default :class:`EvalConfig` allows only ``/tmp/eval-runs`` and\n    ``.dev/eval-runs`` (see ``EvalConfig._default_allowed_scratch_roots``\n    in ``src/superclaude/cli/eval/config.py``); ``~/.claude/`` is not in\n    that set, so :func:`containment_guard`'s check 2 must reject before\n    any hook adapter writes under the per-eval HOME.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def test_setup_refuses_real_dot_claude_as_home_root(\n        self,\n        real_claude_dir: Path,\n        dot_claude_snapshot: _DirSnapshot,\n        cleanup_leaked_eval_homes: None,  # noqa: ARG002 (fixture side effect)\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"``HomeIsolation.setup(home_root=real ~/.claude/, config=default)``\n        MUST raise :class:`HomeContainmentViolation` with\n        ``check='scratch_root_allowlist'``; Before={before_entry!r}, \"\n                f\"after={after_entry!r}.\"\n            )\n\n        # Any newly-created entry MUST be a leaked per-eval HOME with\n        # the test module's eval-id prefix — never anything else.\n        new_entries = set(post.entries) - set(dot_claude_snapshot.entries)\n        leaked_prefix = f\"{_EVAL_ID}-\"\n        for name in new_entries:\n            assert name.startswith(leaked_prefix), (\n                f\"Unexpected new entry {name!r} appeared under ~/.claude/ \"\n                f\"after the refused setup; only entries with the \"\n                f\"{leaked_prefix!r} prefix are accepted as test leaks.\"\n            )\n\n    def test_per_eval_home_is_empty_when_setup_refuses(\n        self,\n        real_claude_dir: Path,\n        cleanup_leaked_eval_homes: None,  # noqa: ARG002 (fixture side effect)\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"The guard fires BEFORE the hook adapter (T02.14) writes\n        anything under the per-eval HOME. When ``setup()`` refuses on\n        the real ``~/.claude/`` case, the leaked per-eval HOME MUST be\n        empty (no nested files, no nested directories, no symlinks).\n\n        This is the second half of NFR-SEC3's \"refusal-before-side-\n        effects\" guarantee: the first half (real ``~/.claude/`` direct\n        children) is asserted by the snapshot test above; this half\n        asserts the per-eval HOME itself.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        iso = HomeIsolation(\n            eval_id=_EVAL_ID,\n            home_root=real_claude_dir,\n            session_id=\"sess-nfrsec3-empty\",\n        )\n\n        with pytest.raises(HomeContainmentViolation):\n            iso.setup(config=EvalConfig())\n\n        # Find the leaked per-eval HOME (mkdtemp prefix + suffix).\n        leaked_prefix = f\"{_EVAL_ID}-\"\n        leaks = [\n            child\n            for child in real_claude_dir.iterdir()\n            if child.name.startswith(leaked_prefix)\n        ]\n        # There MUST be exactly one leak: a single mkdtemp ran before\n        # the guard rejected. We\n        assert that calling :meth:`setup` against ``~/.claude/`` with\n        an *empty* allowlist (the strictest possible config) still\n        rejects via the allowlist check — proving the failure surface\n        is the allowlist comparison, not a side-effect of the default\n        list happening to omit ``~/.claude/``.\n\n        T06.03 (DOC-OQ8) reserves a follow-on \"absolute hard guard\"\n        analysis that would refuse ``~/.claude/`` even when it IS in\n        the allowlist; This makes it safe to\n26\tshare across threads inside the parallel orchestrator (R-058 / T03.16)\n27\twithout locking. They mutate process-local filesystem state (and an internal\n56\tprivate slot tracking the created HOME path) without ever mutating the\n57\tfour DM-006 fields:\n58\t\n59\t* :meth:`setup`                 — ``mkdtemp`` under ``home_root`` and stash\n60\t  the created path on a private ``_home_path`` slot via\n61\t  ``object.__setattr__``. Returns the created path. Idempotency is one-\n62\t  shot per instance: calling :meth:`setup` twice raises ``RuntimeError``\n63\t  rather than silently leaking a directory.\n64\t* :meth:`env`                   — returns the env-var dict the subprocess\n65\t  needs: ``HOME`` always, ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` always, and\n66\t  ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` only when ``time_offset_sec != 0``. The\n67\t  return type pins ``dict[str, str]`` so callers can ``.update()`` it\n68\t  alongside ``IsolationLayers.env_vars`` without coercion.\n69\t* :meth:`teardown`              — removes the per-eval HOME when ``keep``\n70\t  is false, leaves it on disk when ``keep`` is true (so post-mortem\n71\t  inspection or NFR-ISO2 ``setup_failed`` tagging in T02.13 can still\n72\t  pick the directory up). The tag is written under the per-eval HOME so that the\n116\t# directory becomes self-describing for downstream consumers (the orchestrator,\n117\t# the EvalRunner, post-mortem operators): a HOME that carries this relative\n118\t# path is one whose setup raised AFTER ``tempfile.mkdtemp`` materialized the\n119\t# directory and BEFORE ``setup`` returned successfully. The constant is\n120\t# exported so :class:`EvalRunner` (T03.x) and forensics tooling can locate the\n121\t# tag without re-deriving the path.\n122\tSETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH = \".eval-meta/setup_failed\"\n123\t\n124\t\n125\tdef _write_setup_failed_tag(home_path: Path, exc: BaseException) -> None:\n126\t    \"\"\"Write the NFR-ISO2 ``setup_failed`` artifact tag under ``home_path``.\n127\t\n128\t    The tag distinguishes \"harness bug or environmental refusal during\n129\t    setup\" (status ERRORED) from \"eval ran and produced a failing\n130\t    assertion\" (status FAIL). The contents are split for grep-ability:\n131\t\n132\t    * Line 1 is the exception class name verbatim\n133\t      (``type(exc).__name__``), so a downstream EvalRunner can bucket\n134\t      the error with a single ``head -n1`` read.\n135\t    * The remainder is the formatted traceback for forensic inspection.\n136\t\n137\t    The helper is best-effort: ``setup`` catches and discards any\n138\t    exception this function raises so a write failure (e.g. the\n139\t    ``.eval-meta`` directory cannot be created because the per-eval HOME\n140\t    was made read-only by a future hardening step) never masks the\n141\t    original setup exception.\n142\t\n143\t    Args:\n144\t        home_path: The per-eval HOME ``mkdtemp``-ed by\n145\t            :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup`. The tag is materialized at\n146\t            ``home_path / SETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH``.\n147\t        exc: The exception that interrupted ``setup``. Used only to\n148\t            populate the tag contents; this helper never re-raises it.\n149\t    \"\"\"\n150\t\n151\t    tag_path = home_path / SETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH\n152\t    tag_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n153\t    # ``traceback.format_exception`` reads the active exception state when\n154\t    # the helper is invoked from inside an ``except`` clause. Pin the\n155\t    # arguments explicitly so the helper also works when called outside\n156\t    # one (defensive — the only call site is currently inside ``except``).\n157\t    tb_lines = traceback.format_exception(type(exc), exc, exc.__traceback__)\n158\t    payload = f\"{type(exc).__name__}\\n\" + \"\".join(tb_lines)\n159\t    tag_path.write_text(payload, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n160\t\n161\t\n162\tclass HomeContainmentViolation(Exception):\n163\t    \"\"\"Raised by :func:`containment_guard` when any FR-ISO2 check fails.\n164\t\n165\t    The exception is the single hard-failure surface of FR-ISO2 — it is\n166\t    the only thing :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` may raise once\n167\t    :func:`tempfile.mkdtemp` has succeeded and BEFORE the hook adapter\n168\t    (T02.14) writes a single byte under the per-eval HOME. Catching this\n169\t    type at the orchestrator level is sufficient to cover all three\n170\t    containment checks (eval_id regex, scratch-root allowlist, post-\n171\t    mkdtemp symlink resolution); callers MUST NOT branch on the\n172\t    underlying :class:`InvalidEvalId` / :class:`ScratchRootViolation`\n173\t    causes — they are exposed via ``__cause__`` for forensics only.\n174\t\n175\t    The exception MUST raise before any hook deploy or eval-state\n176\t    write so NFR-SEC2 (\"hard refusal before side effects\") is upheld;\n177\t    the NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) is responsible for\n178\t    preserving the partial per-eval HOME with a ``setup_failed`` tag\n179\t    once it lands.\n180\t\n181\t    Attributes:\n182\t        check: Short identifier for the failed check (``\"eval_id\"``,\n183\t            ``\"scratch_root_allowlist\"``, ``\"home_path_resolution\"`` or\n184\t            ``\"home_path_escape\"``). Lets reporters bucket failures\n185\t            without parsing the human-readable message.\n186\t        home_path: The per-eval HOME path the guard was asked to\n187\t            validate. Stored verbatim as supplied by the caller (no\n188\t            resolution) so forensics keep the original input.\n189\t        scratch_root: The declared scratch root the guard checked\n190\t            ``home_path`` against. Stored verbatim.\n191\t        eval_id: The eval id under inspection. Stored verbatim\n192\t            (``repr``-rendered) so non-string smuggled scalars are\n193\t            visible in reporter output.\n194\t        detail: Free-form one-line message describing the specific\n195\t            failure. Inherits from the underlying cause when one\n196\t            exists (e.g. the ``ScratchRootViolation`` rendering).\n197\t    \"\"\"\n198\t\n199\t    def __init__(\n200\t        self,\n201\t        *,\n202\t        check: str,\n203\t        home_path: Path,\n204\t        scratch_root: Path,\n205\t        eval_id: object,\n206\t        detail: str,\n207\t    ) -> None:\n208\t        self.check = check\n209\t        self.home_path = home_path\n210\t        self.scratch_root = scratch_root\n211\t        self.eval_id = eval_id\n212\t        self.detail = detail\n213\t        super().__init__(\n214\t            f\"FR-ISO2 containment check {check!r} failed for \"\n215\t            f\"eval_id={eval_id!r} home_path={home_path!s} \"\n216\t            f\"scratch_root={scratch_root!s}: {detail}\"\n217\t        )\n218\t\n219\t\n220\tdef containment_guard(\n221\t    home_path: Path,\n222\t    scratch_root: Path,\n223\t    eval_id: str,\n224\t    *,\n225\t    config: EvalConfig,\n226\t) -> None:\n227\t    \"\"\"FR-ISO2 three-check defense-in-depth guard.\n228\t\n229\t    The guard layers three independent checks on top of the per-eval\n230\t    HOME materialization that :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` just performed.\n231\t    Each check has a distinct attack vector in scope:\n232\t\n233\t    1. **eval_id regex** — re-applies :func:`validate_eval_id` (T01.05).\n234\t       The loader (T01.07) already validates the id pre-expand and\n235\t       post-expand, but a caller bypassing :class:`SuiteLoader` (e.g.,\n236\t       a programmatic test, a future REPL entry point) could construct\n237\t       a :class:`HomeIsolation` directly. The re-check closes that\n238\t       loader-bypass surface called out in NFR-SEC2.\n239\t    2. **Scratch-root allowlist** — re-applies\n240\t       :func:`resolve_scratch_root` (T01.19) against ``scratch_root``.\n241\t       The helper resolves the path with ``Path.resolve(strict=False)``\n242\t       which collapses symlinks; if ``scratch_root`` is a symlink to a\n243\t       non-allowlisted target (e.g., ``/home/user/.claude``) the\n244\t       resolved form will not match any entry in the resolved\n245\t       :attr:`EvalConfig.allowed_scratch_roots`. The allowlist is\n246\t       sourced exclusively from the caller-supplied\n247\t       :class:`EvalConfig`; nothing inside this module synthesizes a\n248\t       fallback that could inject the caller's ``scratch_root``\n249\t       into the allowlist after the fact (NFR-SEC2 loader-bypass\n250\t       defense — see D-0029 spec rationale).\n251\t    3. **Post-mkdtemp path containment** — resolves ``home_path`` with\n252\t       ``Path.resolve(strict=True)`` and asserts the resolved form is\n253\t       equal to OR a sub-path of the resolved ``scratch_root``. The\n254\t       ``strict=True`` mode raises :class:`FileNotFoundError` if the\n255\t       per-eval HOME has not been materialized yet, so this is the\n256\t       check that pins \"guard runs AFTER ``mkdtemp``\". Catches the\n257\t       attack vector where a symlinked component inside ``home_path``\n258\t       points outside ``scratch_root`` even though the textual prefix\n259\t       check would have passed.\n260\t\n261\t    On any failure the function raises :class:`HomeContainmentViolation`\n262\t    with the failing check identifier. Underlying causes\n263\t    (:class:`InvalidEvalId`, :class:`ScratchRootViolation`,\n264\t    :class:`FileNotFoundError`) are chained via ``raise ... from`` so\n265\t    forensic tooling can still walk ``__cause__``.\n266\t\n267\t    Args:\n268\t        home_path: Path to the per-eval HOME directory ``mkdtemp``-ed by\n269\t            :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup`. Must exist on disk; the\n270\t            ``strict=True`` resolve enforces this so a caller cannot\n271\t            invoke the guard \"ahead\" of ``mkdtemp``.\n272\t        scratch_root: Scratch root under which ``home_path`` must\n273\t            reside. Need not exist (``strict=False`` resolution); for\n274\t            test ergonomics callers can pass a path that\n275\t            :class:`HomeIsolation` will create via\n276\t            ``mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)`` shortly after.\n277\t        eval_id: The eval identifier the per-eval HOME corresponds to.\n278\t            Re-validated through :func:`validate_eval_id`.\n279\t        config: Required :class:`EvalConfig`. Its\n280\t            ``allowed_scratch_roots`` is the sole source of truth for\n281\t            the scratch-root allowlist check. The guard refuses to\n282\t            synthesize a fallback config: a missing argument is a\n283\t            programming error, never a \"use the defaults\" cue. This\n284\t            closes the bypass where a caller could otherwise construct\n285\t            ``HomeIsolation(home_root=<attacker-controlled>)`` and have\n286\t            the guard quietly add that root to the allowlist.\n287\t\n288\t    Raises:\n289\t        HomeContainmentViolation: Any of the three checks failed. The\n290\t            ``check`` attribute identifies which one; ``detail`` carries\n291\t            the human-readable cause; ``__cause__`` holds the underlying\n292\t            exception when applicable.\n293\t    \"\"\"\n294\t\n295\t    # Check 1: eval_id regex. Loader-bypass defense.\n296\t    try:\n297\t        validate_eval_id(eval_id)\n298\t    except InvalidEvalId as exc:\n299\t        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n300\t            check=\"eval_id\",\n301\t            home_path=home_path,\n302\t            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n303\t            eval_id=eval_id,\n304\t            detail=str(exc),\n305\t        ) from exc\n306\t\n307\t    # Check 2: scratch-root allowlist (symlink-aware via resolve_scratch_root).\n308\t    # ``config`` is required: see docstring rationale (NFR-SEC2 bypass close).\n309\t    try:\n310\t        resolved_scratch = resolve_scratch_root(scratch_root, config=config)\n311\t    except ScratchRootViolation as exc:\n312\t        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n313\t            check=\"scratch_root_allowlist\",\n314\t            home_path=home_path,\n315\t            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n316\t            eval_id=eval_id,\n317\t            detail=str(exc),\n318\t        ) from exc\n319\t\n320\t    # Check 3: post-mkdtemp symlink resolution + containment under\n321\t    # scratch_root. strict=True asserts the HOME exists (i.e., mkdtemp\n322\t    # already ran). Any symlink inside home_path is collapsed; the\n323\t    # resolved form must equal or live beneath the resolved scratch root.\n324\t    try:\n325\t        resolved_home = home_path.resolve(strict=True)\n326\t    except FileNotFoundError as exc:\n327\t        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n328\t            check=\"home_path_resolution\",\n329\t            home_path=home_path,\n330\t            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n331\t            eval_id=eval_id,\n332\t            detail=(\n333\t                \"home_path does not exist on disk; FR-ISO2 requires the \"\n334\t                \"guard to run AFTER mkdtemp materialized the per-eval \"\n335\t                \"HOME\"\n336\t            ),\n337\t        ) from exc\n338\t\n339\t    if not (\n340\t        resolved_home == resolved_scratch\n341\t        or resolved_home.is_relative_to(resolved_scratch)\n342\t    ):\n343\t        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n344\t            check=\"home_path_escape\",\n345\t            home_path=home_path,\n346\t            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n347\t            eval_id=eval_id,\n348\t            detail=(\n349\t                f\"resolved home_path {resolved_home!s} escapes resolved \"\n350\t                f\"scratch_root {resolved_scratch!s}\"\n351\t            ),\n352\t        )\n353\t\n354\t\n355\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n356\tclass HomeIsolation:\n357\t    \"\"\"Per-eval HOME isolation record + COMP-006 method surface.\n358\t\n359\t    DM-006 fixes the immutable four-field record contract:\n360\t\n361\t    * ``eval_id``        — ``str``, required. Re-validated in\n362\t      ``__post_init__`` via :func:`validate_eval_id` so any unsafe id\n363\t      raises :class:`superclaude.cli.eval.InvalidEvalId` BEFORE any\n364\t      consumer can derive a filesystem path from it.\n365\t    * ``home_root``      — ``pathlib.Path``, required. Scratch root under\n366\t      which the per-eval HOME is created by :meth:`setup`. The dataclass\n367\t      stores it as-is; full containment is FR-ISO2's responsibility\n368\t      (T02.08).\n369\t    * ``session_id``     — ``str``, required. Value :meth:`env` stamps\n370\t      into ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` for the subprocess. Allocation lives in\n371\t      the orchestrator (FR-G2 / T03.16); this record only holds the\n372\t      assigned value.\n373\t    * ``time_offset_sec`` — ``int``, defaults to ``0``. Optional simulated\n374\t      wall-clock offset in whole seconds; :meth:`env` emits\n375\t      ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` only when non-zero. Full activation is\n376\t      gated on OQ-8 (DOC-OQ8 / T06.03).\n377\t\n378\t    The four declared fields are frozen — mutation raises\n379\t    ``dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError``. A single private slot,\n380\t    ``_home_path``, tracks the post-:meth:`setup` HOME directory; it is\n381\t    written via ``object.__setattr__`` (the documented escape hatch for\n382\t    frozen dataclasses) and never participates in equality or hashing.\n383\t    \"\"\"\n384\t\n385\t    eval_id: str\n386\t    home_root: Path\n387\t    session_id: str\n388\t    time_offset_sec: int = 0\n389\t\n390\t    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n391\t        \"\"\"Re-apply the FR-SCH2 guard and initialize the private path slot.\n392\t\n393\t        The FR-SCH2 re-validation makes loader-bypass impossible:\n394\t        constructing a ``HomeIsolation`` with an unsafe ``eval_id``\n395\t        raises :class:`InvalidEvalId` before any filesystem operation\n396\t        becomes reachable. The private ``_home_path`` slot is set to\n397\t        ``None`` so :meth:`env` / :meth:`state_path` can raise a clear\n398\t        error when called before :meth:`setup`.\n399\t        \"\"\"\n400\t\n401\t        validate_eval_id(self.eval_id)\n402\t        object.__setattr__(self, \"_home_path\", None)\n403\t\n404\t    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n405\t    # COMP-006 method surface (FR-ISO1)\n406\t    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n407\t\n408\t    @property\n409\t    def home_path(self) -> Path:\n410\t        \"\"\"Return the per-eval HOME directory created by :meth:`setup`.\n411\t\n412\t        Raises :class:`RuntimeError` when :meth:`setup` has not yet been\n413\t        called or when :meth:`teardown` has already cleared the slot,\n414\t        so the failure mode is \"obvious AttributeError\" instead of a\n415\t        silent ``None`` flowing into an env dict.\n416\t        \"\"\"\n417\t\n418\t        home = self._home_path  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n435\t\n436\t    def setup(self, *, config: EvalConfig) -> Path:\n437\t        \"\"\"Create the per-eval HOME directory under ``home_root``.\n438\t\n439\t        Uses :func:`tempfile.mkdtemp` with the eval_id as prefix so\n440\t        sibling per-eval HOMEs under the same ``home_root`` are\n441\t        guaranteed to be unique by the OS (atomic on POSIX, see\n442\t        ``mkdtemp`` docs). Parallel evals never collide on directory\n443\t        names; mutating one HOME cannot affect siblings because each\n444\t        HOME is its own ``mkdtemp`` root.\n445\t\n446\t        After ``mkdtemp`` succeeds, :func:`containment_guard` (FR-ISO2,\n447\t        T02.08) is applied AFTER the per-eval HOME has been\n448\t        materialized and BEFORE any hook deploy or eval-state write.\n449\t        The guard re-validates ``eval_id`` against the FR-SCH2 regex,\n450\t        re-applies the AC12 scratch-root allowlist, and\n451\t        ``Path.resolve(strict=True)``-checks the freshly created HOME\n452\t        against the scratch root. Any failure surfaces as\n453\t        :class:`HomeContainmentViolation`; the partial per-eval HOME is\n454\t        left on disk so the NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) can\n455\t        tag it as ``setup_failed`` for forensic inspection.\n456\t\n457\t        Idempotency rule: calling :meth:`setup` twice on the same\n458\t        instance raises :class:`RuntimeError`. This prevents the silent\n459\t        directory leak that would otherwise occur if a caller looped\n460\t        over an instance, and it makes the atomic-setup wrapper\n461\t        (T02.13) easier to reason about.\n462\t\n463\t        Args:\n464\t            config: Required :class:`EvalConfig`. Its\n465\t                ``allowed_scratch_roots`` provides the scratch-root\n466\t                allowlist :func:`containment_guard` enforces.\n467\t                ``setup`` deliberately refuses to synthesize a\n468\t                fallback that injects ``self.home_root`` into the\n469\t                allowlist — that would defeat the AC12 check, because a\n470\t                caller can construct ``HomeIsolation`` with any\n471\t                ``home_root`` (per-instance, the dataclass cannot\n472\t                meaningfully validate it). Production callers\n473\t                (orchestrator T03.16) pass the run's resolved config;\n474\t                tests build an :class:`EvalConfig` whose allowlist\n475\t                includes the test scratch root.\n476\t\n477\t        Returns:\n478\t            The freshly created per-eval HOME path (also recorded on\n479\t            :attr:`home_path`).\n480\t\n481\t        Raises:\n482\t            RuntimeError: If :meth:`setup` was already called on this\n483\t                instance and :meth:`teardown` has not run since.\n484\t            HomeContainmentViolation: Any of the three FR-ISO2 checks\n485\t                failed; the per-eval HOME directory was created and is\n486\t                preserved on disk for inspection with a\n487\t                ``setup_failed`` tag (NFR-ISO2 — see below).\n488\t\n489\t        NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup contract (T02.13 / D-0033)\n490\t        ------------------------------------------------\n491\t\n492\t        Any exception raised AFTER :func:`tempfile.mkdtemp` and BEFORE\n493\t        ``setup`` returns successfully MUST leave the per-eval HOME on\n494\t        disk and MUST drop a ``setup_failed`` artifact tag at\n495\t        ``<home>/.eval-meta/setup_failed`` (constant\n496\t        :data:`SETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH`). The tag's first line is the\n497\t        exception class name; the body is the formatted traceback. The\n498\t        private ``_home_path`` slot stays populated so a caller that\n499\t        wraps ``setup`` in ``try/finally`` can route teardown through\n500\t        ``teardown(keep=True)`` to preserve the tagged HOME for\n501\t        forensics.\n502\t\n503\t        The wrapper distinguishes harness bugs / environmental refusal\n504\t        (status ERRORED — tag present, no eval output) from real eval\n505\t        failures (status FAIL — eval ran, expectations missed). The\n506\t        tag write is best-effort: a secondary exception during tag\n507\t        write is discarded so the original exception is the one the\n508\t        caller sees. The original exception type is preserved\n509\t        verbatim — the wrapper adds the tag side effect, it does not\n510\t        re-wrap or re-classify the exception.\n511\t        \"\"\"\n512\t\n513\t        if self._home_path is not None:  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n514\t            raise RuntimeError(\n515\t                f\"HomeIsolation.setup() already called for eval_id={self.eval_id!r}\"\n516\t            )\n517\t\n518\t        # Ensure the scratch root exists. ``parents=True, exist_ok=True``\n519\t        # is safe because the FR-ISO2 guard below catches any path that\n520\t        # resolves outside the policy allowlist.\n521\t        self.home_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n522\t\n523\t        home = Path(\n524\t            tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f\"{self.eval_id}-\", dir=str(self.home_root))\n525\t        )\n526\t        object.__setattr__(self, \"_home_path\", home)\n527\t\n528\t        # NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper. Anything that raises beyond this\n529\t        # line — FR-ISO2 containment_guard today, any future post-mkdtemp\n530\t        # work (hook deploy verification, capability stamping) tomorrow —\n531\t        # leaves the per-eval HOME on disk with a ``setup_failed`` tag so\n532\t        # the orchestrator can distinguish ERRORED from FAIL. The slot\n533\t        # stays populated so ``teardown(keep=True)`` preserves the\n534\t        # tagged HOME without a re-resolve.\n535\t        try:\n536\t            # FR-ISO2 defense-in-depth: re-check eval_id, scratch-root\n537\t            # allowlist, and symlink-resolved containment AFTER mkdtemp\n538\t            # and BEFORE the hook adapter (T02.14) writes anything under\n539\t            # the per-eval HOME.\n540\t            containment_guard(\n541\t                home_path=home,\n542\t                scratch_root=self.home_root,\n543\t                eval_id=self.eval_id,\n544\t                config=config,\n545\t            )\n546\t        except Exception as exc:\n547\t            # Best-effort tag write. A secondary failure here MUST NOT\n548\t            # mask the original exception; the orchestrator will still\n549\t            # see the partial HOME and can bucket the eval as ERRORED\n550\t            # via the ``home_path`` itself even when the tag is missing.\n551\t            try:\n552\t                _write_setup_failed_tag(home, exc)\n553\t            except Exception:\n554\t                pass\n555\t            raise\n556\t        return home\n557\t\n558\t    def env(self) -> dict[str, str]:\n559\t        \"\"\"Return the env-var dict the subprocess needs for HOME isolation.\n560\t\n561\t        * ``HOME`` is always present.\n562\t        * ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` is always present (FR-ISO1 mandate).\n563\t        * ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` is present only when\n564\t          ``time_offset_sec`` differs from the default ``0``. Keeping the\n565\t          variable opt-in means OQ-8 (DOC-OQ8 / T06.03) does not have to\n566\t          be resolved before this method is safe to call: a caller that\n567\t          does not request a time offset gets the historic two-key dict.\n568\t\n569\t        Callers ``.update()`` this dict alongside\n570\t        ``IsolationLayers.env_vars`` to assemble the full subprocess\n571\t        env; the return type is pinned to ``dict[str, str]`` so the\n572\t        merge is type-clean.\n573\t\n574\t        Raises :class:`RuntimeError` if invoked before :meth:`setup`,\n575\t        because the ``HOME`` value would otherwise have to be a\n576\t        fabricated placeholder.\n577\t        \"\"\"\n578\t\n579\t        env: dict[str, str] = {\n580\t            \"HOME\": str(self.home_path),\n581\t            \"CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\": self.session_id,\n582\t        }\n583\t        if self.time_offset_sec != 0:\n584\t            env[\"CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET\"] = str(self.time_offset_sec)\n585\t        return env\n586\t\n587\t    def teardown(self, keep: bool) -> None:\n588\t        \"\"\"Tear down the per-eval HOME directory.\n589\t\n590\t        * ``keep=False`` removes the directory tree (``shutil.rmtree``\n591\t          with ``ignore_errors=False``; any failure surfaces to the\n592\t          caller so the orchestrator can decide whether to flag the\n593\t          eval as cleanup-failed).\n594\t        * ``keep=True`` leaves the directory on disk for post-mortem\n595\t          inspection — this is the branch the NFR-ISO2 atomic setup\n596\t          wrapper (T02.13) takes when ``setup`` partially fails, and the\n597\t          branch :meth:`teardown` is called on under ``--keep-home``\n598\t          (T03.18).\n599\t\n600\t        Either way, the private ``_home_path`` slot is cleared after\n601\t        teardown, so subsequent :meth:`env` / :meth:`state_path` calls\n602\t        fail loudly instead of pointing at a stale (possibly deleted)\n603\t        path. Calling :meth:`teardown` when no HOME has been set up is\n604\t        a no-op — callers are not required to track setup state\n605\t        themselves.\n606\t        \"\"\"\n607\t\n608\t        home = self._home_path  # type: ignore[attr-defined]\n609\t        if home is None:\n610\t            return  # No-op: setup never ran, or teardown already ran.\n611\t\n612\t        try:\n613\t            if not keep:\n614\t                shutil.rmtree(home)\n615\t        finally:\n616\t            # Always clear the slot — even if rmtree raised, the path\n617\t            # is no longer trustworthy for future env() / state_path()\n618\t            # consumers and callers will re-run setup() if they want a\n619\t            # fresh HOME.\n620\t            object.__setattr__(self, \"_home_path\", None)\n621\t\n622\t    def state_path(self, suffix: str) -> Path:\n623\t        \"\"\"Return ``home_path / suffix`` after a light containment check.\n624\t\n625\t        ``suffix`` must be a relative path (no leading separator) and\n626\t        must not contain ``..`` components — otherwise the returned\n627\t        path could escape ``home_path`` and undermine the\n628\t        per-eval HOME isolation. Full FR-ISO2 containment\n629\t        (symlink resolution + allowlist) lands in T02.08; this method\n630\t        performs only the minimum check necessary to keep the\n631\t        primitive safe in isolation.\n632\t\n633\t        Raises :class:`ValueError` for any suffix that escapes\n634\t        ``home_path``. Raises :class:`RuntimeError` if invoked before\n635\t        :meth:`setup` (via :attr:`home_path`).\n636\t        \"\"\"\n637\t\n638\t        candidate = Path(suffix)\n639\t        if candidate.is_absolute():\n640\t            raise ValueError(\n641\t                f\"state_path suffix must be relative, got absolute path: {suffix!r}\"\n642\t            )\n643\t        if \"..\" in candidate.parts:\n644\t            raise ValueError(\n645\t                f\"state_path suffix must not contain '..' components: {suffix!r}\"\n646\t            )\n647\t\n648\t        joined = self.home_path / candidate\n649\t        # Defense-in-depth: even with the part check above, refuse any\n650\t        # joined path that does not resolve under home_path. The COMP-012 probe at\n``tests/cli/eval/test_isolation_layers_probe.py`` (Task T02.05) must keep\npassing after this extension, which it does because nothing in\n``cli/sprint/executor.py`` is touched.\n\nDM-006 record (T02.04) — the four immutable fields\n====================================================\n\n``HomeIsolation`` is a ``@dataclass(frozen=True)`` so once an instance is\nbuilt it cannot be mutated by downstream consumers. This makes it safe to\nshare across threads inside the parallel orchestrator (R-058 / T03.16)\nwithout locking. Equality is structural across all four fields via the\ndataclass-generated ``__eq__``.\n\n1. ``eval_id``         — the FR-SCH2-validated eval identifier. The id\n   flows directly into the per-eval HOME by :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup`\n   so it MUST satisfy the FR-SCH2 regex before any filesystem write. The\n   ``__post_init__`` hook re-applies :func:`validate_eval_id` (T01.05) so\n   a ``HomeIsolation`` can never be constructed with an unsafe id even\n   when a caller bypasses the loader.\n2. ``home_root``       — the scratch root directory under which the\n   per-eval HOME is ``mkdtemp``-ed by :meth:`setup`. Full FR-ISO2 path\n   containment lands in T02.08; this module performs only the minimal\n   ``parents=True, exist_ok=True`` materialization needed to create\n   sibling per-eval HOMEs concurrently.\n3. ``session_id``      — the value :meth:`env` stamps into\n   ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID``. Allocation lives in the orchestrator (FR-G2 /\n   T03.16); this record only holds the assigned value.\n4. ``time_offset_sec`` — optional offset in whole seconds for the eval's\n   simulated wall clock. Default ``0`` matches DM-006 verbatim; when the\n   value is non-zero :meth:`env` adds ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET``. Whether\n   the subprocess actually honors the variable is gated on OQ-8 (DOC-OQ8\n   / T06.03); keeping the offset opt-in via non-zero values means the\n   env dict stays minimal until OQ-8 resolves.\n\nCOMP-006 method surface (T02.07) — FR-ISO1\n==========================================\n\nThe four methods below are the externally observable surface of\nCOMP-006. They mutate process-local filesystem state (and an internal\nprivate slot tracking the created HOME path) without ever mutating the\nfour DM-006 fields:\n\n* :meth:`setup`                 — ``mkdtemp`` under ``home_root`` and stash\n  the created path on a private ``_home_path`` slot via\n  ``object.__setattr__``. Returns the created path. Idempotency is one-\n  shot per instance: calling :meth:`setup` twice raises ``RuntimeError``\n  rather than silently leaking a directory.\n* :meth:`env`                   — returns the env-var dict the subprocess\n  needs: ``HOME`` always, ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` always, and\n  ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`` only when ``time_offset_sec != 0``. The\n  return type pins ``dict[str, str]`` so callers can ``.update()`` it\n  alongside ``IsolationLayers.env_vars`` without coercion.\n* :meth:`teardown`              — removes the per-eval HOME when ``keep``\n  is false, leaves it on disk when ``keep`` is true (so post-mortem\n  inspection or NFR-ISO2 ``setup_failed`` tagging in T02.13 can still\n  pick the directory up). Always clears the private path slot so the\n  instance is safe to garbage-collect.\n* :meth:`state_path`            — joins ``suffix`` under the active\n  per-eval HOME so consumers (hook adapter T02.14, atomic setup T02.13,\n  artifact writers) never need to import :mod:`os.path` themselves. The tag is written under the per-eval HOME so that the\n# directory becomes self-describing for downstream consumers (the orchestrator,\n# the EvalRunner, post-mortem operators): a HOME that carries this relative\n# path is one whose setup raised AFTER ``tempfile.mkdtemp`` materialized the\n# directory and BEFORE ``setup`` returned successfully. The constant is\n# exported so :class:`EvalRunner` (T03.x) and forensics tooling can locate the\n# tag without re-deriving the path.\nSETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH = \".eval-meta/setup_failed\"\n\n\ndef _write_setup_failed_tag(home_path: Path, exc: BaseException) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Write the NFR-ISO2 ``setup_failed`` artifact tag under ``home_path``.\n\n    The tag distinguishes \"harness bug or environmental refusal during\n    setup\" (status ERRORED) from \"eval ran and produced a failing\n    assertion\" (status FAIL). The contents are split for grep-ability:\n\n    * Line 1 is the exception class name verbatim\n      (``type(exc).__name__``), so a downstream EvalRunner can bucket\n      the error with a single ``head -n1`` read.\n    * The remainder is the formatted traceback for forensic inspection.\n\n    The helper is best-effort: ``setup`` catches and discards any\n    exception this function raises so a write failure (e.g. the\n    ``.eval-meta`` directory cannot be created because the per-eval HOME\n    was made read-only by a future hardening step) never masks the\n    original setup exception.\n\n    Args:\n        home_path: The per-eval HOME ``mkdtemp``-ed by\n            :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup`. The tag is materialized at\n            ``home_path / SETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH``.\n        exc: The exception that interrupted ``setup``. Used only to\n            populate the tag contents; this helper never re-raises it.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    tag_path = home_path / SETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH\n    tag_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    # ``traceback.format_exception`` reads the active exception state when\n    # the helper is invoked from inside an ``except`` clause. Pin the\n    # arguments explicitly so the helper also works when called outside\n    # one (defensive — the only call site is currently inside ``except``).\n    tb_lines = traceback.format_exception(type(exc), exc, exc.__traceback__)\n    payload = f\"{type(exc).__name__}\\n\" + \"\".join(tb_lines)\n    tag_path.write_text(payload, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\nclass HomeContainmentViolation(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised by :func:`containment_guard` when any FR-ISO2 check fails.\n\n    The exception is the single hard-failure surface of FR-ISO2 — it is\n    the only thing :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` may raise once\n    :func:`tempfile.mkdtemp` has succeeded and BEFORE the hook adapter\n    (T02.14) writes a single byte under the per-eval HOME. Catching this\n    type at the orchestrator level is sufficient to cover all three\n    containment checks (eval_id regex, scratch-root allowlist, post-\n    mkdtemp symlink resolution); callers MUST NOT branch on the\n    underlying :class:`InvalidEvalId` / :class:`ScratchRootViolation`\n    causes — they are exposed via ``__cause__`` for forensics only.\n\n    The exception MUST raise before any hook deploy or eval-state\n    write so NFR-SEC2 (\"hard refusal before side effects\") is upheld;\n    the NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) is responsible for\n    preserving the partial per-eval HOME with a ``setup_failed`` tag\n    once it lands.\n\n    Attributes:\n        check: Short identifier for the failed check (``\"eval_id\"``,\n            ``\"scratch_root_allowlist\"``, ``\"home_path_resolution\"`` or\n            ``\"home_path_escape\"``). Lets reporters bucket failures\n            without parsing the human-readable message.\n        home_path: The per-eval HOME path the guard was asked to\n            validate. Stored verbatim as supplied by the caller (no\n            resolution) so forensics keep the original input.\n        scratch_root: The declared scratch root the guard checked\n            ``home_path`` against. Stored verbatim.\n        eval_id: The eval id under inspection. The re-check closes that\n       loader-bypass surface called out in NFR-SEC2.\n    2. **Scratch-root allowlist** — re-applies\n       :func:`resolve_scratch_root` (T01.19) against ``scratch_root``.\n       The helper resolves the path with ``Path.resolve(strict=False)``\n       which collapses symlinks; if ``scratch_root`` is a symlink to a\n       non-allowlisted target (e.g., ``/home/user/.claude``) the\n       resolved form will not match any entry in the resolved\n       :attr:`EvalConfig.allowed_scratch_roots`. The allowlist is\n       sourced exclusively from the caller-supplied\n       :class:`EvalConfig`; nothing inside this module synthesizes a\n       fallback that could inject the caller's ``scratch_root``\n       into the allowlist after the fact (NFR-SEC2 loader-bypass\n       defense — see D-0029 spec rationale).\n    3. **Post-mkdtemp path containment** — resolves ``home_path`` with\n       ``Path.resolve(strict=True)`` and asserts the resolved form is\n       equal to OR a sub-path of the resolved ``scratch_root``. The\n       ``strict=True`` mode raises :class:`FileNotFoundError` if the\n       per-eval HOME has not been materialized yet, so this is the\n       check that pins \"guard runs AFTER ``mkdtemp``\". Catches the\n       attack vector where a symlinked component inside ``home_path``\n       points outside ``scratch_root`` even though the textual prefix\n       check would have passed.\n\n    On any failure the function raises :class:`HomeContainmentViolation`\n    with the failing check identifier. Underlying causes\n    (:class:`InvalidEvalId`, :class:`ScratchRootViolation`,\n    :class:`FileNotFoundError`) are chained via ``raise ... from`` so\n    forensic tooling can still walk ``__cause__``.\n\n    Args:\n        home_path: Path to the per-eval HOME directory ``mkdtemp``-ed by\n            :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup`. Must exist on disk; the\n            ``strict=True`` resolve enforces this so a caller cannot\n            invoke the guard \"ahead\" of ``mkdtemp``.\n        scratch_root: Scratch root under which ``home_path`` must\n            reside. Need not exist (``strict=False`` resolution); for\n            test ergonomics callers can pass a path that\n            :class:`HomeIsolation` will create via\n            ``mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)`` shortly after.\n        eval_id: The eval identifier the per-eval HOME corresponds to.\n            Re-validated through :func:`validate_eval_id`.\n        config: Required :class:`EvalConfig`. Its\n            ``allowed_scratch_roots`` is the sole source of truth for\n            the scratch-root allowlist check. The guard refuses to\n            synthesize a fallback config: a missing argument is a\n            programming error, never a \"use the defaults\" cue. This\n            closes the bypass where a caller could otherwise construct\n            ``HomeIsolation(home_root=<attacker-controlled>)`` and have\n            the guard quietly add that root to the allowlist.\n\n    Raises:\n        HomeContainmentViolation: Any of the three checks failed. The\n            ``check`` attribute identifies which one; ``detail`` carries\n            the human-readable cause; ``__cause__`` holds the underlying\n            exception when applicable.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Check 1: eval_id regex. Loader-bypass defense.\n    try:\n        validate_eval_id(eval_id)\n    except InvalidEvalId as exc:\n        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n            check=\"eval_id\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n            eval_id=eval_id,\n            detail=str(exc),\n        ) from exc\n\n    # Check 2: scratch-root allowlist (symlink-aware via resolve_scratch_root).\n    # ``config`` is required: see docstring rationale (NFR-SEC2 bypass close).\n    try:\n        resolved_scratch = resolve_scratch_root(scratch_root, config=config)\n    except ScratchRootViolation as exc:\n        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n            check=\"scratch_root_allowlist\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n            eval_id=eval_id,\n            detail=str(exc),\n        ) from exc\n\n    # Check 3: post-mkdtemp symlink resolution + containment under\n    # scratch_root. strict=True asserts the HOME exists (i.e., mkdtemp\n    # already ran). Any symlink inside home_path is collapsed; the\n    # resolved form must equal or live beneath the resolved scratch root.\n    try:\n        resolved_home = home_path.resolve(strict=True)\n    except FileNotFoundError as exc:\n        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n            check=\"home_path_resolution\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n            eval_id=eval_id,\n            detail=(\n                \"home_path does not exist on disk; FR-ISO2 requires the \"\n                \"guard to run AFTER mkdtemp materialized the per-eval \"\n                \"HOME\"\n            ),\n        ) from exc\n\n    if not (\n        resolved_home == resolved_scratch\n        or resolved_home.is_relative_to(resolved_scratch)\n    ):\n        raise HomeContainmentViolation(\n            check=\"home_path_escape\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            scratch_root=scratch_root,\n            eval_id=eval_id,\n            detail=(\n                f\"resolved home_path {resolved_home!s} escapes resolved \"\n                f\"scratch_root {resolved_scratch!s}\"\n            ),\n        )\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass HomeIsolation:\n    \"\"\"Per-eval HOME isolation record + COMP-006 method surface.\n\n    DM-006 fixes the immutable four-field record contract:\n\n    * ``eval_id``        — ``str``, required. Re-validated in\n      ``__post_init__`` via :func:`validate_eval_id` so any unsafe id\n      raises :class:`superclaude.cli.eval.InvalidEvalId` BEFORE any\n      consumer can derive a filesystem path from it.\n    * ``home_root``      — ``pathlib.Path``, required. Scratch root under\n      which the per-eval HOME is created by :meth:`setup`. Parallel evals never collide on directory\n        names; mutating one HOME cannot affect siblings because each\n        HOME is its own ``mkdtemp`` root.\n\n        After ``mkdtemp`` succeeds, :func:`containment_guard` (FR-ISO2,\n        T02.08) is applied AFTER the per-eval HOME has been\n        materialized and BEFORE any hook deploy or eval-state write.\n        The guard re-validates ``eval_id`` against the FR-SCH2 regex,\n        re-applies the AC12 scratch-root allowlist, and\n        ``Path.resolve(strict=True)``-checks the freshly created HOME\n        against the scratch root. Any failure surfaces as\n        :class:`HomeContainmentViolation`; the partial per-eval HOME is\n        left on disk so the NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13) can\n        tag it as ``setup_failed`` for forensic inspection.\n\n        Idempotency rule: calling :meth:`setup` twice on the same\n        instance raises :class:`RuntimeError`. This prevents the silent\n        directory leak that would otherwise occur if a caller looped\n        over an instance, and it makes the atomic-setup wrapper\n        (T02.13) easier to reason about.\n\n        Args:\n            config: Required :class:`EvalConfig`.
